🗣 量化甲 Let me read the detailed rules on market dynamics and accounts before designing a strategy.
◈ 量化甲 第 2 次调用
[1·行动] 量化甲 ◇ 查询规则 主题=PAMS 交互市场 → 【PAMS 交互市场】
市场用 PAMS 0.3.0 订单簿撮合，买卖改变盘口与后续成交；同批匹配按统一清算价成交。每个交易日20000逻辑tick，计算机尽快运行，不等待现实20分钟。同tick公共快照一致，委托和撤单最早下一tick生效；市价单有2%价格保护，未成交量到期。price参数可指定限价，Day限价单可部分成交，收盘撤销；orders/fills只返回自己的委托和成交。价格由有限资金与库存的背景做市者、基本面代理、噪声流及玩家委托共同形成。基本面受市场与行业因子驱动，波动有平静和风暴状态；每只股票的波动、贝塔、消息敏感度、价格吸收速度和盘口流动性都不同（见quotes的volatility与depth）。公开消息六类：市场快讯（宏观，全行业按贝塔传导）、公司快讯（盘中错峰发布）、定期财报（每5个交易日一轮，日程公开见next_report_round，内容事先未知）、公司事件（监管/突破/事故/做市/政策，会永久改变该股的波动率、贝塔、流动性等个性，变化随新闻公开）与上市/退市公告。股票池是动态的：新股按种子排定的日程陆续挂牌（pool()公布下一只的名称、行业、发行价与回合，首只在第7个交易日，之后每6个交易日一只，初始10只+候选4只）；退市规则公开可算：收盘价连续5个交易日低于发行锚价（见quotes的anchor）的35%发风险警示，再5个交易日复核，仍低于则按收盘价5%折价（空头溢价）场外强制回购后摘牌；股票池下限8只。退市股票从行情消失，脚本引用会报"没有这只股票"，请用pool()或quotes核对最新名单。quotes() 每只股票返回：symbol 代码、name 名称、sector 行业、anchor 发行锚价、price 最新价、prev_close 昨收、change_pct 涨跌幅、open 今开、path 今天已走过的价格序列（约 20 点）、tick 当前 tick、ma5/ma10/ma20 均线、momentum_5 近5点动量、volatility 近10点波动率、commission_rate 佣金率、float_shares 流通股、depth 盘口 {bids/asks 各档价位与数量}、borrow_available 可借券数、report_cycle_days/next_report_round/report_tick 财报日程、delist_review_round 退市复核、news 该股最近消息。catalog() 只给代码/名称/行业。历史全部保留、随时可查：history(symbol, limit=30，最多256) 返回每天收盘价，从旧到新；ticks(symbol, limit=200，最多2000，round=可选回合号) 返回盘中采样（每 200 tick 一点、每天约 100 点，整局保留），每点 {round, tick, price}。orders()/fills()/day_summary() 只返回自己的委托、成交与昨日回执。quotes 不含种子、未来消息或未来价格。可用buy/sell开平多头，short/cover开平空头；做空受借券、保证金和借券费约束。
[1·行动] 量化甲 ◇ 查询规则 主题=账户、借贷与多空保证金 → 【账户、借贷与多空保证金】
初始现金 100000。净值=现金+多头市值−空头市值−现金借款。现金借款每回合利息 2%，只从现金扣一次。买卖成交立即入账；挂单先冻结现金、股票或保证金，撤单、到期释放。buy/sell 是开多/平多，short/cover 是借券卖空/买回还券，同股票先平反向仓位。借券有限，卖空收入及额外 50% 保证金受限；维持保证金 30%，每日借券费 0.02%。风控会撤风险挂单、使用可用现金还债，再由清算银行按标记价及清算折价接盘减仓；清算会实际扣付现金和转移股票，净值非正则破产。算力费用计入净值。
[1·行动] 量化甲 ◇ 查询规则 主题=Python 策略与研究槽 → 【Python 策略与研究槽】
script(action=write/patch/read/status,slot="1"..."4"或"research")管理各槽源码；read 免费查看自己的当前完整源码；每次写入或修订后自动检查并返回结果，失败的源码仍保存，须修好或续买算力才能运行；普通源码只在交易日自动执行；休市日交易槽不运行，研究槽仍可手动运行。定义无参数 def on_tick(): 会每100tick重新回调，可用api.call("stock:clock","sleep",ticks=N)设置下一次回调间隔，不暂停、不推进行情、不按等待扣步数。回调必须返回，不能用while+sleep等待；单次回调上限 = 50000计算单位、200次API × 算力池档数（撞上限时买池或拆到多个交易槽）。脚本语法树节点上限随算力池档数提升：第1/2/3/4档为2048/4096/6144/8192，交易槽与研究槽均按当前档位检查；源码检查与执行字节上限依档位为16384/32768/49152/65536；允许保存未超65536字节的源码，执行仍受当前档限制。算力池每回合重置，次日未续买则上限也恢复，超限源码仍保存但不能执行。state是每槽独立、跨回调和跨日保存的64KB JSON字典；成功回调提交state，失败不撤销已受理动作。行情与账户一律用 api.call(能力, 方法, ...)，签名如下："stock:market"：quotes()、history(symbol, limit=30)、ticks(symbol)、catalog()、pool()、orders()、fills()、day_summary()、buy(symbol, quantity, price=可选限价)、sell(...)、short(...)、cover(...)、cancel(order_id)；"stock:portfolio"：holdings()、equity_curve()、borrow(amount)、repay(amount)；"stock:clock"：snapshot()、sleep(ticks=100)；"stock:compute"：status()；"stock:intelligence"：status()。symbol 取 quotes() 返回的 symbol 字段；参数写错时回执会给出该方法的完整签名和本能力可用方法。股票池是动态的：新股按公开日程挂牌、崩坏的股票会被警示后退市，写策略前先 pool() 核对名单。普通源码（没有 on_tick）在开盘 tick 0 执行一次，适合开盘建仓；定义 on_tick 则默认每 100 tick 回调，可用 sleep(ticks=N) 改间隔。每个tick所有回调读同一公共时间截面，订单下一tick生效。最小可用示例（槽1，均线趋势）：
def on_tick():
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for row in book["positions"]:
        held[row["symbol"]] = row["quantity"]
    for row in quotes:
        symbol = row["symbol"]
        if row["ma5"] is None or row["ma20"] is None:
            continue
        if row["ma5"] > row["ma20"] and not held.get(symbol):
            if book["available_cash"] > 20000:
                api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=symbol, quantity=100)
        elif held.get(symbol) and row["ma5"] < row["ma20"]:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=symbol, quantity=held[symbol])
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=100)
研究槽在行动时手动运行 script(action=run,slot="research",inputs={...})：每次固定 200 现金、每回合最多 3 次，只读、不下单、不推进行情，也不占共享算力池（交易槽 1～4 不能手动 run，只在交易日结束时自动执行）。研究单次上限 100 万步、1000 次 API。print 每槽每天合计上限4096字节，只打印摘要（len、前几条、关键字段），不要打印整个dict。沙盒是 Python 子集：可写赋值、if/elif/else、for/while、break/continue/pass、def（无装饰器/注解/可变参数）、return、列表与字典推导；没有 try/except/finally、class、import、with、lambda、raise、assert、global、f-string、集合字面量、set/generator 推导。排序用 def 具名函数作 sorted/min/max 的 key=；字符串用 + 拼接或 % 格式化。内置函数仅限 abs all any bool dict enumerate float int len list max min print range reversed round sorted str sum tuple zip；对象方法仅限 get items keys values append extend pop copy count index sort reverse lower upper strip split join startswith endswith replace；属性不能赋值，双下划线名字禁用。被拒的写法会在报错信息里附一句改法；每次写入和修订自动检查。print、错误行与各槽用量会出现在状态和下回合简报。禁止导入、文件、网络、随机和动态执行。
🗣 量化甲 I have a clear picture now. Let me think about the approach:

- 60 rounds total, final round auto-liquidates everything to cash. I need to grow cash over ~42 trading days.
- Day 1 has no price history (all indicators flat), so I'll start with a simple cross-sectional momentum strategy, collect data, then refine using the research slot.
- Commission is 0.1%/side, so I need hysteresis to avoid churn.
- No need to buy compute/slots yet — 1 slot and 4M steps are plenty for a light strategy.

Let me write a conservative first strategy to slot 1.

🗣 量化甲 The strategy is saved and ready. It's a conservative cross-sectional momentum rotation: rebalances 5×/day, holds the top-6 momentum names, deploys ~45,000 of 100,000 cash across the top 3 (leaving a large cash buffer), and sells any holding that drops out of the top 6. On day 1 all momentum is flat so it will establish small initial positions; I'll gather data and refine with the research slot in coming rounds.

No need to buy extra compute or slots yet — one slot and the free tier are plenty for this light strategy.

### 第 1 回合 · 量化乙 行动

🗣 量化乙 I'll start by understanding the game mechanics more deeply and checking the current state. Let me query the rules and key status in parallel.
◈ 量化乙 第 1 次调用
[1·行动] 量化乙 ◇ 查询规则 主题=all → 【游戏目标与回合】
【怎么赢】最终回合开始时，现金最多的人赢。股票、空头、未还贷款都不直接算分：最后一回合系统会撤掉所有未成交委托、按最后成交价平掉全部多头和空头（照收佣金）、再用现金尽量归还贷款；结算现金 = 现金 − 仍未还清的贷款，按结算现金排名。所以越接近终局，越应该把仓位换成现金，别把风险留到最后一刻。【回合】本局共 60 回合；第 N 回合不行动，只做清算。随时用 round 工具看 round（当前第几回合）、final_round（最终回合）、rounds_left（还剩几回合）。每 5 个交易日后休市 2 天，休市日不撮合，但仍可规划、研究和租用资源。【一回合怎么过】① 盘前规划：查行情、写/改策略脚本、买算力或情报、借贷，每账户最多 40 次规划动作；② end_turn 提交一句小结；③ 全体结束后锁定策略，交易日行情沿 20000 个逻辑 tick 推进并自动执行交易槽；休市日不运行交易槽；委托最早下一 tick 生效；④ 收盘结算费用、利息与风险，进入下一回合，简报会给你脚本输出与成交结果。【第一回合做什么】先看 round（还剩几回合）、portfolio（现金与持仓）、market（行情与盘口）、compute_status（算力预算）；写一份保守的小仓位策略到槽 1（先用研究槽验证字段更稳），再 end_turn。【重要】模型工具里没有买卖按钮，所有交易都必须写成脚本；sleep 只安排下次回调，不推进行情。

【算力池与计算槽】
【免费档】每回合免费 4000000 计算单位、4000 次 API、1 个自动交易槽。【每回合重买】算力池和交易槽只在本回合有效，新回合自动归零，需要重新购买：
  算力池第 2/3/4 档：120 / 240 / 480 现金每回合，每档 +4000000 计算单位、+4000 次 API；
  交易槽第 2/3/4 个：80 / 160 / 320 现金每回合，每多一个槽就多一份可并行的策略。
compute(action=buy,kind=pool|slot) 是当回合购买，compute(action=release,...) 退租不退钱。算力池档数同时决定单次回调上限：第 N 档 = 50000 步 / 200 次 API 的 N 倍。【研究槽】不需要购买，按次收费：每次 script(action=run,slot="research") 固定 200 现金，每回合最多 3 次；研究不占共享算力池，单次上限见规则「Python 策略与研究槽」。研究槽只能读行情、账户和已买报告，不能下单、借贷或推进行情。

【商业谍报】
intel(action=steal,target=玩家id)支付1000游戏现金，确定获得对方调用时的现金、可用现金、银行贷款、多空市值及各股票持仓数量快照；报告注明回合、tick和阶段。intel(action=source,target=玩家id)支付2500游戏现金，从对方已保存源码的全部交易槽/研究槽中随机挑一个，再随机截取连续至多6行源码；报告注明槽位、源码版本与行号范围（第X～Y行/共N行）。同目标重复偷取会得到不同片段，对方修订后版本号变化；源码很短时可能整段被偷走。intel_status列出公开玩家名单及自己最近取得的报告。报告不会随对手后续操作自动更新。不能偷自己、背景做市商或还没保存过源码的目标；普通API仍只看自己。研究和交易脚本只能读取已购买报告，不能在脚本里发动谍报。费用计入净值及商业谍报费用。

【PAMS 交互市场】
市场用 PAMS 0.3.0 订单簿撮合，买卖改变盘口与后续成交；同批匹配按统一清算价成交。每个交易日20000逻辑tick，计算机尽快运行，不等待现实20分钟。同tick公共快照一致，委托和撤单最早下一tick生效；市价单有2%价格保护，未成交量到期。price参数可指定限价，Day限价单可部分成交，收盘撤销；orders/fills只返回自己的委托和成交。价格由有限资金与库存的背景做市者、基本面代理、噪声流及玩家委托共同形成。基本面受市场与行业因子驱动，波动有平静和风暴状态；每只股票的波动、贝塔、消息敏感度、价格吸收速度和盘口流动性都不同（见quotes的volatility与depth）。公开消息六类：市场快讯（宏观，全行业按贝塔传导）、公司快讯（盘中错峰发布）、定期财报（每5个交易日一轮，日程公开见next_report_round，内容事先未知）、公司事件（监管/突破/事故/做市/政策，会永久改变该股的波动率、贝塔、流动性等个性，变化随新闻公开）与上市/退市公告。股票池是动态的：新股按种子排定的日程陆续挂牌（pool()公布下一只的名称、行业、发行价与回合，首只在第7个交易日，之后每6个交易日一只，初始10只+候选4只）；退市规则公开可算：收盘价连续5个交易日低于发行锚价（见quotes的anchor）的35%发风险警示，再5个交易日复核，仍低于则按收盘价5%折价（空头溢价）场外强制回购后摘牌；股票池下限8只。退市股票从行情消失，脚本引用会报"没有这只股票"，请用pool()或quotes核对最新名单。quotes() 每只股票返回：symbol 代码、name 名称、sector 行业、anchor 发行锚价、price 最新价、prev_close 昨收、change_pct 涨跌幅、open 今开、path 今天已走过的价格序列（约 20 点）、tick 当前 tick、ma5/ma10/ma20 均线、momentum_5 近5点动量、volatility 近10点波动率、commission_rate 佣金率、float_shares 流通股、depth 盘口 {bids/asks 各档价位与数量}、borrow_available 可借券数、report_cycle_days/next_report_round/report_tick 财报日程、delist_review_round 退市复核、news 该股最近消息。catalog() 只给代码/名称/行业。历史全部保留、随时可查：history(symbol, limit=30，最多256) 返回每天收盘价，从旧到新；ticks(symbol, limit=200，最多2000，round=可选回合号) 返回盘中采样（每 200 tick 一点、每天约 100 点，整局保留），每点 {round, tick, price}。orders()/fills()/day_summary() 只返回自己的委托、成交与昨日回执。quotes 不含种子、未来消息或未来价格。可用buy/sell开平多头，short/cover开平空头；做空受借券、保证金和借券费约束。

【账户、借贷与多空保证金】
初始现金 100000。净值=现金+多头市值−空头市值−现金借款。现金借款每回合利息 2%，只从现金扣一次。买卖成交立即入账；挂单先冻结现金、股票或保证金，撤单、到期释放。buy/sell 是开多/平多，short/cover 是借券卖空/买回还券，同股票先平反向仓位。借券有限，卖空收入及额外 50% 保证金受限；维持保证金 30%，每日借券费 0.02%。风控会撤风险挂单、使用可用现金还债，再由清算银行按标记价及清算折价接盘减仓；清算会实际扣付现金和转移股票，净值非正则破产。算力费用计入净值。

【Python 策略与研究槽】
script(action=write/patch/read/status,slot="1"..."4"或"research")管理各槽源码；read 免费查看自己的当前完整源码；每次写入或修订后自动检查并返回结果，失败的源码仍保存，须修好或续买算力才能运行；普通源码只在交易日自动执行；休市日交易槽不运行，研究槽仍可手动运行。定义无参数 def on_tick(): 会每100tick重新回调，可用api.call("stock:clock","sleep",ticks=N)设置下一次回调间隔，不暂停、不推进行情、不按等待扣步数。回调必须返回，不能用while+sleep等待；单次回调上限 = 50000计算单位、200次API × 算力池档数（撞上限时买池或拆到多个交易槽）。脚本语法树节点上限随算力池档数提升：第1/2/3/4档为2048/4096/6144/8192，交易槽与研究槽均按当前档位检查；源码检查与执行字节上限依档位为16384/32768/49152/65536；允许保存未超65536字节的源码，执行仍受当前档限制。算力池每回合重置，次日未续买则上限也恢复，超限源码仍保存但不能执行。state是每槽独立、跨回调和跨日保存的64KB JSON字典；成功回调提交state，失败不撤销已受理动作。行情与账户一律用 api.call(能力, 方法, ...)，签名如下："stock:market"：quotes()、history(symbol, limit=30)、ticks(symbol)、catalog()、pool()、orders()、fills()、day_summary()、buy(symbol, quantity, price=可选限价)、sell(...)、short(...)、cover(...)、cancel(order_id)；"stock:portfolio"：holdings()、equity_curve()、borrow(amount)、repay(amount)；"stock:clock"：snapshot()、sleep(ticks=100)；"stock:compute"：status()；"stock:intelligence"：status()。symbol 取 quotes() 返回的 symbol 字段；参数写错时回执会给出该方法的完整签名和本能力可用方法。股票池是动态的：新股按公开日程挂牌、崩坏的股票会被警示后退市，写策略前先 pool() 核对名单。普通源码（没有 on_tick）在开盘 tick 0 执行一次，适合开盘建仓；定义 on_tick 则默认每 100 tick 回调，可用 sleep(ticks=N) 改间隔。每个tick所有回调读同一公共时间截面，订单下一tick生效。最小可用示例（槽1，均线趋势）：
def on_tick():
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for row in book["positions"]:
        held[row["symbol"]] = row["quantity"]
    for row in quotes:
        symbol = row["symbol"]
        if row["ma5"] is None or row["ma20"] is None:
            continue
        if row["ma5"] > row["ma20"] and not held.get(symbol):
            if book["available_cash"] > 20000:
                api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=symbol, quantity=100)
        elif held.get(symbol) and row["ma5"] < row["ma20"]:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=symbol, quantity=held[symbol])
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=100)
研究槽在行动时手动运行 script(action=run,slot="research",inputs={...})：每次固定 200 现金、每回合最多 3 次，只读、不下单、不推进行情，也不占共享算力池（交易槽 1～4 不能手动 run，只在交易日结束时自动执行）。研究单次上限 100 万步、1000 次 API。print 每槽每天合计上限4096字节，只打印摘要（len、前几条、关键字段），不要打印整个dict。沙盒是 Python 子集：可写赋值、if/elif/else、for/while、break/continue/pass、def（无装饰器/注解/可变参数）、return、列表与字典推导；没有 try/except/finally、class、import、with、lambda、raise、assert、global、f-string、集合字面量、set/generator 推导。排序用 def 具名函数作 sorted/min/max 的 key=；字符串用 + 拼接或 % 格式化。内置函数仅限 abs all any bool dict enumerate float int len list max min print range reversed round sorted str sum tuple zip；对象方法仅限 get items keys values append extend pop copy count index sort reverse lower upper strip split join startswith endswith replace；属性不能赋值，双下划线名字禁用。被拒的写法会在报错信息里附一句改法；每次写入和修订自动检查。print、错误行与各槽用量会出现在状态和下回合简报。禁止导入、文件、网络、随机和动态执行。

🗣 量化乙 I have a clear picture now. Let me think about the strategy:

- 60 rounds total, final round liquidates everything to cash → need consistent returns
- 0.1% commission per trade → avoid overtrading
- All stocks start flat at anchor; volatility emerges during the day
- Need a robust cross-sectional strategy with hysteresis to limit turnover

🗣 量化乙 The strategy is saved and validated. Let me review my approach before ending the turn:

**Strategy rationale:**
- **Momentum + mean-reversion hybrid**: `score = 5×(ma5-ma20)/ma20 + (ma5-price)/ma5` — captures trend strength with a slight pullback entry bonus
- **Conservative exposure**: max 4 positions × ~10,000 each = ~40% of capital, keeping 60% in cash
- **Hysteresis via keep set**: top 6 by score are kept, only top 4 bought — avoids churn from rank jitter
- **Sell losers, buy winners**: rebalance every 2000 ticks (10 callbacks/day) to limit commission drag
- **No leverage on day 1**: learning the market dynamics first

I'll end the turn now and observe the results.

[1·行动] 量化丙 ◇ 查询规则 主题=PAMS 交互市场 → 【PAMS 交互市场】
市场用 PAMS 0.3.0 订单簿撮合，买卖改变盘口与后续成交；同批匹配按统一清算价成交。每个交易日20000逻辑tick，计算机尽快运行，不等待现实20分钟。同tick公共快照一致，委托和撤单最早下一tick生效；市价单有2%价格保护，未成交量到期。price参数可指定限价，Day限价单可部分成交，收盘撤销；orders/fills只返回自己的委托和成交。价格由有限资金与库存的背景做市者、基本面代理、噪声流及玩家委托共同形成。基本面受市场与行业因子驱动，波动有平静和风暴状态；每只股票的波动、贝塔、消息敏感度、价格吸收速度和盘口流动性都不同（见quotes的volatility与depth）。公开消息六类：市场快讯（宏观，全行业按贝塔传导）、公司快讯（盘中错峰发布）、定期财报（每5个交易日一轮，日程公开见next_report_round，内容事先未知）、公司事件（监管/突破/事故/做市/政策，会永久改变该股的波动率、贝塔、流动性等个性，变化随新闻公开）与上市/退市公告。股票池是动态的：新股按种子排定的日程陆续挂牌（pool()公布下一只的名称、行业、发行价与回合，首只在第7个交易日，之后每6个交易日一只，初始10只+候选4只）；退市规则公开可算：收盘价连续5个交易日低于发行锚价（见quotes的anchor）的35%发风险警示，再5个交易日复核，仍低于则按收盘价5%折价（空头溢价）场外强制回购后摘牌；股票池下限8只。退市股票从行情消失，脚本引用会报"没有这只股票"，请用pool()或quotes核对最新名单。quotes() 每只股票返回：symbol 代码、name 名称、sector 行业、anchor 发行锚价、price 最新价、prev_close 昨收、change_pct 涨跌幅、open 今开、path 今天已走过的价格序列（约 20 点）、tick 当前 tick、ma5/ma10/ma20 均线、momentum_5 近5点动量、volatility 近10点波动率、commission_rate 佣金率、float_shares 流通股、depth 盘口 {bids/asks 各档价位与数量}、borrow_available 可借券数、report_cycle_days/next_report_round/report_tick 财报日程、delist_review_round 退市复核、news 该股最近消息。catalog() 只给代码/名称/行业。历史全部保留、随时可查：history(symbol, limit=30，最多256) 返回每天收盘价，从旧到新；ticks(symbol, limit=200，最多2000，round=可选回合号) 返回盘中采样（每 200 tick 一点、每天约 100 点，整局保留），每点 {round, tick, price}。orders()/fills()/day_summary() 只返回自己的委托、成交与昨日回执。quotes 不含种子、未来消息或未来价格。可用buy/sell开平多头，short/cover开平空头；做空受借券、保证金和借券费约束。
[1·行动] 量化丙 ◇ 查询规则 主题=Python 策略与研究槽 → 【Python 策略与研究槽】
script(action=write/patch/read/status,slot="1"..."4"或"research")管理各槽源码；read 免费查看自己的当前完整源码；每次写入或修订后自动检查并返回结果，失败的源码仍保存，须修好或续买算力才能运行；普通源码只在交易日自动执行；休市日交易槽不运行，研究槽仍可手动运行。定义无参数 def on_tick(): 会每100tick重新回调，可用api.call("stock:clock","sleep",ticks=N)设置下一次回调间隔，不暂停、不推进行情、不按等待扣步数。回调必须返回，不能用while+sleep等待；单次回调上限 = 50000计算单位、200次API × 算力池档数（撞上限时买池或拆到多个交易槽）。脚本语法树节点上限随算力池档数提升：第1/2/3/4档为2048/4096/6144/8192，交易槽与研究槽均按当前档位检查；源码检查与执行字节上限依档位为16384/32768/49152/65536；允许保存未超65536字节的源码，执行仍受当前档限制。算力池每回合重置，次日未续买则上限也恢复，超限源码仍保存但不能执行。state是每槽独立、跨回调和跨日保存的64KB JSON字典；成功回调提交state，失败不撤销已受理动作。行情与账户一律用 api.call(能力, 方法, ...)，签名如下："stock:market"：quotes()、history(symbol, limit=30)、ticks(symbol)、catalog()、pool()、orders()、fills()、day_summary()、buy(symbol, quantity, price=可选限价)、sell(...)、short(...)、cover(...)、cancel(order_id)；"stock:portfolio"：holdings()、equity_curve()、borrow(amount)、repay(amount)；"stock:clock"：snapshot()、sleep(ticks=100)；"stock:compute"：status()；"stock:intelligence"：status()。symbol 取 quotes() 返回的 symbol 字段；参数写错时回执会给出该方法的完整签名和本能力可用方法。股票池是动态的：新股按公开日程挂牌、崩坏的股票会被警示后退市，写策略前先 pool() 核对名单。普通源码（没有 on_tick）在开盘 tick 0 执行一次，适合开盘建仓；定义 on_tick 则默认每 100 tick 回调，可用 sleep(ticks=N) 改间隔。每个tick所有回调读同一公共时间截面，订单下一tick生效。最小可用示例（槽1，均线趋势）：
def on_tick():
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for row in book["positions"]:
        held[row["symbol"]] = row["quantity"]
    for row in quotes:
        symbol = row["symbol"]
        if row["ma5"] is None or row["ma20"] is None:
            continue
        if row["ma5"] > row["ma20"] and not held.get(symbol):
            if book["available_cash"] > 20000:
                api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=symbol, quantity=100)
        elif held.get(symbol) and row["ma5"] < row["ma20"]:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=symbol, quantity=held[symbol])
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=100)
研究槽在行动时手动运行 script(action=run,slot="research",inputs={...})：每次固定 200 现金、每回合最多 3 次，只读、不下单、不推进行情，也不占共享算力池（交易槽 1～4 不能手动 run，只在交易日结束时自动执行）。研究单次上限 100 万步、1000 次 API。print 每槽每天合计上限4096字节，只打印摘要（len、前几条、关键字段），不要打印整个dict。沙盒是 Python 子集：可写赋值、if/elif/else、for/while、break/continue/pass、def（无装饰器/注解/可变参数）、return、列表与字典推导；没有 try/except/finally、class、import、with、lambda、raise、assert、global、f-string、集合字面量、set/generator 推导。排序用 def 具名函数作 sorted/min/max 的 key=；字符串用 + 拼接或 % 格式化。内置函数仅限 abs all any bool dict enumerate float int len list max min print range reversed round sorted str sum tuple zip；对象方法仅限 get items keys values append extend pop copy count index sort reverse lower upper strip split join startswith endswith replace；属性不能赋值，双下划线名字禁用。被拒的写法会在报错信息里附一句改法；每次写入和修订自动检查。print、错误行与各槽用量会出现在状态和下回合简报。禁止导入、文件、网络、随机和动态执行。
[1·行动] 量化丙 ◇ 查询规则 主题=账户、借贷与多空保证金 → 【账户、借贷与多空保证金】
初始现金 100000。净值=现金+多头市值−空头市值−现金借款。现金借款每回合利息 2%，只从现金扣一次。买卖成交立即入账；挂单先冻结现金、股票或保证金，撤单、到期释放。buy/sell 是开多/平多，short/cover 是借券卖空/买回还券，同股票先平反向仓位。借券有限，卖空收入及额外 50% 保证金受限；维持保证金 30%，每日借券费 0.02%。风控会撤风险挂单、使用可用现金还债，再由清算银行按标记价及清算折价接盘减仓；清算会实际扣付现金和转移股票，净值非正则破产。算力费用计入净值。

槽 1 当前源码（第 1 版）：
def score_of(r):
    if r["ma5"] is None or r["ma10"] is None or r["ma20"] is None:
        return None
    if r["price"] <= 0 or r["ma20"] <= 0 or r["volatility"] <= 0:
        return None
    trend = r["ma5"] / r["ma20"] - 1.0
    mom = r["momentum_5"] / r["price"]
    chg = r["change_pct"] / 100.0
    return trend + 0.6 * mom + 0.3 * chg

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    if not snap["trading_day"]:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return
    rnd = snap["round"]
    tick = snap["tick"]
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")

    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p["quantity"]

    if rnd >= 57:
        if state.get("derisk") != rnd:
            state["derisk"] = rnd
            for sym, qty in held.items():
                if qty > 0:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)
        return

    if tick < 300 or state.get("day") == rnd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)
        return
    state["day"] = rnd

    scored = []
    for r in quotes:
        s = score_of(r)
        if s is not None:
            scored.append([s, r["symbol"], r["price"]])
    def byscore(a):
        return a[0]
    scored.sort(key=byscore, reverse=True)

    if len(scored) < 5:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)
        return

    rank = {}
    pxmap = {}
    for i, x in enumerate(scored):
        rank[x[1]] = i
        pxmap[x[1]] = x[2]

    for sym, qty in held.items():
        if qty > 0 and rank.get(sym, 999) > 5:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)

    n = 0
    for sym, qty in held.items():
        if qty > 0 and rank.get(sym, 999) <= 5:
            n += 1

    cash = book["available_cash"]
    for x in scored:
        if n >= 3:
            break
        sym = x[1]
        if held.get(sym, 0) > 0:
            continue
        if cash < 12000:
            continue
        px = pxmap[sym]
        qty = int(18000 / px)
        if qty < 1:
            continue
        api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
        cash -= qty * px
        n += 1

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)

🗣 量化丙 The strategy is saved and validated. Let me summarize my approach for this round.

My plan:
- **Slot 1 strategy**: Cross-sectional momentum — score each stock by `ma5/ma20` trend + momentum + daily change, hold top-3, rebalance once daily after tick 300.
- **Risk controls**: Max ~18,000 cash per position, no leverage, no shorting, and forced de-risking from round 57 onward (3 rounds before final liquidation).
- **Day 1**: All stocks have zero volatility/flat MAs at tick 0, so the strategy naturally sits out and gathers data — positions begin from day 2.
- **Compute**: 1 pool / 1 slot is sufficient (~100 callbacks/day, well within 4,000 API calls), so no extra spend needed.

[1·行动] 量化丁 ◇ 读取脚本 → 槽 1 当前源码（第 4 版）：
def on_tick():
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p["quantity"]

    # 更新持仓最高价（移动止盈用）
    highs = state.get("highs", {})
    for r in quotes:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s in held and r["price"] > highs.get(s, 0):
            highs[s] = r["price"]
    state["highs"] = highs

    # 卖出/止损/止盈
    for r in quotes:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s not in held:
            continue
        px = r["price"]
        ma5 = r["ma5"]
        ma10 = r["ma10"]
        ma20 = r["ma20"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        hi = highs.get(s, px)
        # 趋势与动量转弱
        weak = (ma5 is not None and ma10 is not None and ma5 < ma10 and mom < 0)
        # 跌破20日线且动量负
        broken = (ma20 is not None and px < ma20 and mom < 0)
        # 从高点回撤超过8%
        drawdown = (hi > 0 and px < hi * 0.92)
        if weak or broken or drawdown:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=s, quantity=held[s])
            highs[s] = 0
            continue

    # 选股：趋势向上、动量为正、均线多头排列
    cands = []
    for r in quotes:
        s = r["symbol"]
        px = r["price"]
        ma5 = r["ma5"]
        ma10 = r["ma10"]
        ma20 = r["ma20"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        vol = r["volatility"]
        if ma5 is None or ma10 is None or ma20 is None or mom is None:
            continue
        if px <= 0 or vol is None or vol <= 0:
            continue
        if ma5 > ma10 and ma10 >= ma20 and mom > 0 and px >= ma5:
            cands.append(r)

    # 按动量/波动比排序，取前4只
    def score(r):
        return r["momentum_5"] / (r["volatility"] + 0.001)
    cands.sort(key=score, reverse=True)
    cands = cands[:4]

    # 计算目标仓位：总资金的60%，按波动倒数加权
    invest = cash * 0.6
    if cands:
        invsum = 0.0
        for r in cands:
            invsum += 1.0 / (r["volatility"] + 0.005)
        for r in cands:
            s = r["symbol"]
            if s in held:
                continue
            w = (1.0 / (r["volatility"] + 0.005)) / invsum
            amt = invest * w
            qty = int(amt / r["price"])
            if qty < 1:
                continue
            if cash < amt * 1.05:
                continue
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=s, quantity=qty)

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=100)

🗣 量化丁 脚本逻辑完整、检查通过。第1回合所有股票波动率和动量均为0，策略会保持空仓观望——这是合理的，等消息和行情启动后再按趋势信号建仓。API用量（每100tick约2次调用，全天约400次）在免费额度内，无需额外购买算力或情报。

[2·行动] 量化丁 ◇ 读取脚本 → 槽 1 当前源码（第 9 版）：
def on_tick():
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p["quantity"]

    # 更新持仓最高价（移动止盈用）
    highs = state.get("highs", {})
    for r in quotes:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s in held and r["price"] > highs.get(s, 0):
            highs[s] = r["price"]
    state["highs"] = highs

    # 卖出/止损/止盈
    for r in quotes:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s not in held:
            continue
        px = r["price"]
        ma5 = r["ma5"]
        ma10 = r["ma10"]
        ma20 = r["ma20"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        hi = highs.get(s, px)
        # 趋势与动量转弱
        weak = (ma5 is not None and ma10 is not None and ma5 < ma10 and mom < 0)
        # 跌破20日线且动量负
        broken = (ma20 is not None and px < ma20 and mom < 0)
        # 从高点回撤超过8%
        drawdown = (hi > 0 and px < hi * 0.92)
        # 动量转负且跌破5日线
        stalled = (mom < 0 and ma5 is not None and px < ma5)
        if weak or broken or drawdown or stalled:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=s, quantity=held[s])
            highs[s] = 0
            continue

    # 选股：趋势向上、动量为正、均线多头排列
    cands = []
    for r in quotes:
        s = r["symbol"]
        px = r["price"]
        ma5 = r["ma5"]
        ma10 = r["ma10"]
        ma20 = r["ma20"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        vol = r["volatility"]
        if ma5 is None or ma10 is None or ma20 is None or mom is None:
            continue
        if px <= 0 or vol is None or vol <= 0:
            continue
        if ma5 > ma10 and ma10 >= ma20 and mom > 0 and px >= ma5:
            cands.append(r)

    # 按动量/波动比排序，取前4只
    def score(r):
        return r["momentum_5"] / (r["volatility"] + 0.001)
    cands.sort(key=score, reverse=True)
    cands = cands[:4]

    # 读取在途委托，避免重复下单
    pending = []
    for o in api.call("stock:market", "orders"):
        if o.get("status") in ("open", "pending", "new"):
            pending.append(o.get("symbol"))

    # 计算目标仓位：总资金的60%，按波动倒数加权
    invest = cash * 0.6
    if cands:
        invsum = 0.0
        for r in cands:
            invsum += 1.0 / (r["volatility"] + 0.005)
        for r in cands:
            s = r["symbol"]
            if s in held or s in pending:
                continue
            w = (1.0 / (r["volatility"] + 0.005)) / invsum
            amt = invest * w
            qty = int(amt / r["price"])
            if qty < 1:
                continue
            if cash < amt * 1.05:
                continue
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=s, quantity=qty)

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)

考虑到超限会导致全天动作归零（第1回合的教训），多花一点现金买保险完全值得。我再升一档到3档（单次上限15万步），彻底消除风险。

[2·行动] 量化甲 ◇ 读取脚本 → 槽 1 当前源码（第 3 版）：
def on_tick():
    q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p["quantity"]
    rows = []
    for r in q:
        m = r["momentum_5"]
        if m is None:
            m = 0.0
        cf = 0.0
        for n in r["news"]:
            c = n.get("cashflow_change_pct", 0.0)
            if c is not None:
                cf = cf + c
        score = m + 0.005 * cf
        rows.append([r["symbol"], score, r["price"], r["depth"]])
    def ks(a):
        return a[1]
    rows.sort(key=ks, reverse=True)
    keep = []
    for a in rows[:6]:
        keep.append(a[0])
    for sym in held:
        if held[sym] > 0 and sym not in keep:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])
    top = rows[:3]
    for a in top:
        sym = a[0]
        if held.get(sym, 0) > 0 or cash <= 12000:
            continue
        d = a[3]
        bids = d.get("bids", [])
        bid = a[2]
        if len(bids) > 0:
            bid = bids[0][0]
        qty = int(22000.0 / bid)
        if qty > 0:
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty, price=bid)
            cash = cash - qty * bid
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=4000)

🗣 量化丁 诊断清楚了，有两个关键问题：

**问题1：限价单大量expired。** 我用的是市价单（没传price），但市价单有2%价格保护，且盘口深度很浅（每档50-140股）。大单（如yundu 591股、taiping 1180股）远超盘口容量，只吃到零星成交（每笔50-110股）就到期了。这解释了持仓为何零碎。

**问题2：重复下单。** 我的pending检查用 `status in ("open","pending","new")`，但实际状态是 `placed`/`partial` 等，导致检查失效，同一股票被反复下单（yundu出现两次591、572股）。

修复方案：
1. **降低单笔数量**到盘口可吸收的规模（约50-100股），用略高于现价的限价单提高成交率；
2. **修正pending检查**，用实际状态字段；
3. **减少目标股票数**到3只，集中资金。

[3·行动] 量化丁 ◇ 读取脚本 → 槽 1 当前源码（第 11 版）：
def on_tick():
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p["quantity"]

    # 更新持仓最高价
    highs = state.get("highs", {})
    for r in quotes:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s in held and r["price"] > highs.get(s, 0):
            highs[s] = r["price"]
    state["highs"] = highs

    # 活动委托的股票（placed/partial）
    pending = []
    for o in api.call("stock:market", "orders"):
        if o.get("status") in ("placed", "partial", "open", "new"):
            pending.append(o.get("symbol"))

    # 卖出：趋势破坏或止损
    for r in quotes:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s not in held:
            continue
        px = r["price"]
        ma5 = r["ma5"]
        ma10 = r["ma10"]
        ma20 = r["ma20"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        hi = highs.get(s, px)
        weak = (ma5 is not None and ma10 is not None and ma5 < ma10 and mom < 0)
        broken = (ma20 is not None and px < ma20 and mom < 0)
        drawdown = (hi > 0 and px < hi * 0.90)
        stalled = (mom < 0 and ma5 is not None and px < ma5)
        if weak or broken or drawdown or stalled:
            if s not in pending:
                api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=s, quantity=held[s], price=px * 0.99)
                highs[s] = 0

    # 选股：多头排列且动量为正
    cands = []
    for r in quotes:
        ma5 = r["ma5"]
        ma10 = r["ma10"]
        ma20 = r["ma20"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        vol = r["volatility"]
        if ma5 is None or ma10 is None or ma20 is None or mom is None:
            continue
        if r["price"] <= 0 or vol is None or vol <= 0:
            continue
        if ma5 > ma10 and ma10 >= ma20 and mom > 0 and r["price"] >= ma5:
            cands.append(r)

    def score(r):
        return r["momentum_5"] / (r["volatility"] + 0.001)
    cands.sort(key=score, reverse=True)
    cands = cands[:3]

    # 分批建仓：每次每只买80股，限价挂买一价
    for r in cands:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s in pending:
            continue
        # 限价挂在现价下方0.5%，等待回踩成交
        limit_px = r["price"] * 0.995
        qty = 80
        if cash < limit_px * qty * 1.1:
            continue
        api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=s, quantity=qty, price=limit_px)

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)

🗣 量化丁 脚本逻辑完整。但我意识到一个严重问题：**pending检查会阻止加仓**。

因为限价单挂在现价下方0.5%，如果当天没成交，它会一直处于placed状态，导致后续所有回调都跳过这只股票，无法继续建仓。而且已持有的股票（held）虽然不会被重复买入，但新建仓的股票一旦有挂单未成交，就被锁住了。

不过这其实是合理的——避免同一股票堆积过多未成交委托。每天收盘未成交委托会撤销，次日重新来过。40次回调中，只要限价单成交一次，pending就清空，可以再次挂单加仓。

我接受这个设计。提交本回合，观察修复效果。

def on_tick():
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]

    held = {}
    for row in book["positions"]:
        held[row["symbol"]] = row["quantity"]

    scores = []
    for row in quotes:
        sym = row["symbol"]
        p = row["price"]
        ma20 = row["ma20"]
        mom = row["momentum_5"]
        if ma20 is None or mom is None or ma20 <= 0 or p <= 0:
            continue
        trend = (p - ma20) / ma20
        score = trend + 2 * mom
        scores.append([score, sym, p])

    def by_score(a):
        return a[0]

    scores.sort(key=by_score, reverse=True)

    keep = {}
    for i in range(min(7, len(scores))):
        if scores[i][0] > 0.01:
            keep[scores[i][1]] = True

    for sym in held:
        if sym not in keep:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])

    bought = 0
    for i in range(min(5, len(scores))):
        sym = scores[i][1]
        p = scores[i][2]
        if scores[i][0] <= 0.01:
            continue
        if sym in held:
            continue
        if bought >= 5:
            break
        if cash < 15000:
            continue
        qty = int(13000 / p)
        if qty >= 1:
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
            cash = cash - qty * p
            bought = bought + 1

    print("scores=" + str(len(scores)) + " keep=" + str(len(keep)) + " held=" + str(len(held)) + " bought=" + str(bought))
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=4000)

def on_tick():
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    tick = quotes[0]["tick"]

    held = {}
    for row in book["positions"]:
        held[row["symbol"]] = row["quantity"]

    cur_round = state.get("round", 0)
    for row in quotes:
        for n in row["news"]:
            if n["round"] > cur_round:
                cur_round = n["round"]

    if state.get("round", 0) != cur_round:
        state["round"] = cur_round
        state["seen"] = {}
        state["entries"] = {}

    seen = state.get("seen", {})
    entries = state.get("entries", {})

    if not state.get("init", False):
        for sym in held:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])
        state["init"] = True

    if tick >= 19400:
        for sym in held:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])
        state["entries"] = {}
        state["seen"] = {}
        print("EOD sold=" + str(len(held)))
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=100)
        return

    for sym in list(held.keys()):
        if sym in entries and tick - entries[sym] >= 3000:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])
            entries.pop(sym)

    bought = 0
    for row in quotes:
        sym = row["symbol"]
        p = row["price"]
        for n in row["news"]:
            if n["round"] != cur_round:
                continue
            key = str(n["tick"]) + ":" + sym
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen[key] = True
            cf = n["cashflow_change_pct"]
            if cf >= 2.0 and held.get(sym, 0) == 0 and cash > 6000 and bought < 5:
                qty = int(8000 / p)
                if qty >= 1:
                    api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
                    cash = cash - qty * p
                    entries[sym] = tick
                    bought = bought + 1
            elif cf <= -2.0 and held.get(sym, 0) > 0:
                api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])
                if sym in entries:
                    entries.pop(sym)

    state["seen"] = seen
    state["entries"] = entries
    if bought > 0:
        print("BUY tick=" + str(tick) + " n=" + str(bought))
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=100)

**5. 清理僵尸持仓**：qingzhou只有4股，直接清掉。

[4·行动] 量化丁 ◇ 读取脚本 → 槽 1 当前源码（第 14 版）：
def on_tick():
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    wealth = book["wealth"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p

    # 活动委托股票
    pending = []
    for o in api.call("stock:market", "orders"):
        if o.get("status") in ("placed", "partial", "open", "new"):
            pending.append(o.get("symbol"))

    # 卖出：成本止损 + 趋势破坏 + 清理极小仓位
    for r in quotes:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s not in held:
            continue
        p = held[s]
        qty = p["quantity"]
        pnl = p.get("pnl_pct", 0)
        px = r["price"]
        ma5 = r["ma5"]
        ma20 = r["ma20"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        # 浮亏超5%止损
        stop_loss = (pnl is not None and pnl < -5)
        # 极小仓位清理
        tiny = qty < 20
        # 趋势破坏
        broken = (ma20 is not None and px < ma20 and mom is not None and mom < 0)
        # 仓位过度集中：超过25%财富
        overweight = (qty * px > wealth * 0.25)
        if stop_loss or tiny or broken or overweight:
            if s not in pending:
                # 超仓时只卖出超出部分，其余全清
                if overweight and not stop_loss and not tiny and not broken:
                    excess = int((qty * px - wealth * 0.15) / px)
                    if excess > 0:
                        api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=s, quantity=excess, price=px * 0.99)
                else:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=s, quantity=qty, price=px * 0.98)

    # 选股：多头排列、动量为正、波动适中
    cands = []
    for r in quotes:
        ma5 = r["ma5"]
        ma10 = r["ma10"]
        ma20 = r["ma20"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        vol = r["volatility"]
        if ma5 is None or ma10 is None or ma20 is None or mom is None:
            continue
        if r["price"] <= 0 or vol is None or vol <= 0:
            continue
        if ma5 > ma10 and ma10 >= ma20 and mom > 0 and r["price"] >= ma5:
            cands.append(r)

    def score(r):
        return r["momentum_5"] / (r["volatility"] + 0.001)
    cands.sort(key=score, reverse=True)
    cands = cands[:3]

    # 分批建仓：单股目标上限15%财富，每次买60股，限价现价下方0.3%
    for r in cands:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s in pending:
            continue
        cur_val = 0
        if s in held:
            cur_val = held[s]["quantity"] * r["price"]
        target = wealth * 0.15
        if cur_val >= target:
            continue
        limit_px = r["price"] * 0.997
        qty = 60
        # 现金安全：单笔不超过可用现金的15%
        if cash < limit_px * qty or cash < 5000:
            continue
        api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=s, quantity=qty, price=limit_px)
        cash = cash - limit_px * qty

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)

🗣 量化丁 脚本逻辑完整。但我发现一个执行顺序问题：**先卖后买同一股票会亏价差**（提示也提到了）。当前jinhui既超仓需要卖，又可能在cands里被买入。虽然jinhui当前动量0.0069较低、大概率不在前3，但为保险起见，我把今日卖出过的股票排除在买入之外。

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    if not snap["trading_day"]:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return
    rnd = snap["round"]
    tick = snap["tick"]

    if rnd >= 57:
        if state.get("derisk") != rnd:
            state["derisk"] = rnd
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            for p in b["positions"]:
                if p["quantity"] > 0:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=p["symbol"], quantity=p["quantity"])
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)
        return

    if tick < 400 or state.get("day") == rnd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)
        return
    state["day"] = rnd

    q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for p in b["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p["quantity"]

    rows = []
    for r in q:
        h = api.call("stock:market", "history", symbol=r["symbol"], limit=4)
        if len(h) < 4 or h[-4] <= 0:
            continue
        roc = h[-1] / h[-4] - 1.0
        rows.append([roc, r["symbol"], r["price"]])
    def by(a):
        return a[0]
    rows.sort(key=by, reverse=True)

    if len(rows) < 5:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)
        return

    keep = []
    for x in rows[:4]:
        keep.append(x[1])

    for sym, qty in held.items():
        if qty > 0 and sym not in keep:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)

    cash = b["available_cash"]
    for x in rows[:4]:
        sym = x[1]
        if held.get(sym, 0) > 0:
            continue
        if cash < 5000:
            continue
        px = x[2]
        qty = int(4000 / px)
        if qty < 10:
            continue
        api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty, price=px * 1.01)
        cash -= qty * px

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    tick = snap["tick"]
    rnd = snap["round"]
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for row in book["positions"]:
        held[row["symbol"]] = row["quantity"]
    cash = book["available_cash"]

    if state.get("last_round", 0) != rnd:
        state["last_round"] = rnd
        state["seen"] = {}
        state["entries"] = {}
        for sym in held:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])
        print("NEW r=" + str(rnd))
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    prices = {}
    for row in quotes:
        prices[row["symbol"]] = row["price"]

    entries = state.get("entries", {})
    for sym in list(entries.keys()):
        if sym not in held:
            entries.pop(sym)
            continue
        ent = entries[sym]
        cur = prices.get(sym, ent[1])
        if tick - ent[0] >= 1500 or cur < ent[1] * 0.98:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])
            entries.pop(sym)

    if tick >= 19000:
        for sym in held:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])
        state["entries"] = {}
        print("EOD")
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    seen = state.get("seen", {})
    bought = 0
    for row in quotes:
        sym = row["symbol"]
        p = row["price"]
        for n in row["news"]:
            if n["round"] != rnd:
                continue
            key = str(n["tick"]) + ":" + sym
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen[key] = True
            cf = n["cashflow_change_pct"]
            if cf >= 2.5 and held.get(sym, 0) == 0 and cash > 6000 and bought < 3:
                qty = int(6000 / p)
                if qty >= 1:
                    api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
                    cash = cash - qty * p
                    entries[sym] = [tick, p]
                    bought = bought + 1

    state["seen"] = seen
    state["entries"] = entries
    if bought > 0:
        print("B t=" + str(tick) + " n=" + str(bought))
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)

**Confirmed findings:**
- `corr(cumulative news, cumulative return) = 0.975` — news fully explains price levels
- `corr(today's news, today's intraday return) = 0.833` — strong

**Key realization**: The market is highly efficient with news. The only reliable edge is **speed** — reacting to news faster than the price fully adjusts. My 500-tick polling with 5000-size orders was too slow/small. Let me check competitor strategies for any structural edge I'm missing.

[5·行动] 量化丙 ◇ 读取脚本 → 槽 1 当前源码（第 8 版）：
def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    if not snap["trading_day"]:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return
    rnd = snap["round"]
    tick = snap["tick"]

    if rnd >= 57:
        if state.get("derisk") != rnd:
            state["derisk"] = rnd
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            for p in b["positions"]:
                if p["quantity"] > 0:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=p["symbol"], quantity=p["quantity"])
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    if "day" not in state:
        state["day"] = -1
    if "ar" not in state:
        state["ar"] = {}
    if "at" not in state:
        state["at"] = {}
    if "entry" not in state:
        state["entry"] = {}

    q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for p in b["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p["quantity"]
    pxmap = {}
    for r in q:
        pxmap[r["symbol"]] = r["price"]

    if state["day"] != rnd:
        state["day"] = rnd
        for sym, qty in held.items():
            if qty > 0:
                api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
        held = {}

    if tick >= 19000:
        for sym, qty in held.items():
            if qty > 0:
                api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    for sym, qty in held.items():
        if qty <= 0:
            continue
        e = state["entry"].get(sym)
        px = pxmap.get(sym, 0)
        if e is None or px <= 0:
            continue
        if px > e * 1.04 or px < e * 0.97:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
            held[sym] = 0

    npos = 0
    for sym, qty in held.items():
        if qty > 0:
            npos += 1
    cash = b["available_cash"]

    for r in q:
        sym = r["symbol"]
        bt = -1
        bcf = 0.0
        for n in r["news"][-6:]:
            if n["kind"] == "wire" and n["round"] == rnd and n["tick"] <= tick:
                if n["tick"] > bt:
                    bt = n["tick"]
                    bcf = n["cashflow_change_pct"]
        if bt < 0:
            continue
        if state["ar"].get(sym, -1) == rnd and state["at"].get(sym, -1) >= bt:
            continue
        state["ar"][sym] = rnd
        state["at"][sym] = bt
        px = r["price"]
        if bcf > 2.0 and held.get(sym, 0) == 0 and npos < 6 and cash > 9000:
            qty = int(9000 / px)
            if qty >= 10:
                api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty, price=px * 1.012)
                state["entry"][sym] = px
                held[sym] = qty
                npos += 1
                cash -= qty * px
        elif bcf < -2.0 and held.get(sym, 0) > 0:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])
            held[sym] = 0

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)

🗣 量化丙 The strategy is saved correctly. Let me end the turn.

[6·行动] 量化丙 ◇ 读取脚本 → 槽 1 当前源码（第 9 版）：
def byroc(a):
    return a[0]

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    if not snap["trading_day"]:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return
    rnd = snap["round"]
    tick = snap["tick"]

    if rnd >= 57:
        if state.get("derisk") != rnd:
            state["derisk"] = rnd
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            for p in b["positions"]:
                if p["quantity"] > 0:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=p["symbol"], quantity=p["quantity"])
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    if tick < 400 or state.get("day") == rnd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)
        return
    state["day"] = rnd

    q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for p in b["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p["quantity"]

    rows = []
    for r in q:
        h = api.call("stock:market", "history", symbol=r["symbol"], limit=4)
        if len(h) < 4 or h[-4] <= 0:
            continue
        roc = h[-1] / h[-4] - 1.0
        if roc > 0:
            rows.append([roc, r["symbol"], r["price"]])
    rows.sort(key=byroc, reverse=True)

    keep = []
    for x in rows[:6]:
        keep.append(x[1])

    for sym, qty in held.items():
        if qty > 0 and sym not in keep:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)

    cash = b["available_cash"]
    budget = 13000
    for x in rows[:6]:
        sym = x[1]
        if held.get(sym, 0) > 0:
            continue
        if cash < budget + 2000:
            continue
        px = x[2]
        qty = int(budget / px)
        if qty < 10:
            continue
        api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty, price=px * 1.02)
        cash -= qty * px

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)

🗣 量化丙 The strategy is saved correctly. Today is a rest day, so no trading occurs — this was a good opportunity to redesign. Let me end the turn.

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    rd = snap["round"]
    if state.get("day") == rd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=5000)
        return
    state["day"] = rd
    q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p
    rows = []
    for r in q:
        ma = r["ma20"]
        px = r["price"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        if ma is None or ma == 0 or mom is None:
            continue
        score = mom + 0.5 * (px / ma - 1.0)
        rows.append([r["symbol"], score, px, r["depth"]])
    def ks(a):
        return a[1]
    rows.sort(key=ks, reverse=True)
    top5 = []
    for a in rows[:5]:
        top5.append(a[0])
    nkeep = 0
    for sym in held:
        if sym in top5:
            nkeep = nkeep + 1
    for sym in held:
        p = held[sym]
        aq = p["available_quantity"]
        if aq <= 0:
            continue
        if sym not in top5 or p["pnl_pct"] < -6.0:
            px = p["price"]
            for a in rows:
                if a[0] == sym:
                    px = a[2]
            sellpx = px * 0.95
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=aq, price=sellpx)
    slots = 4 - nkeep
    if slots < 0:
        slots = 0
    for a in rows:
        if slots <= 0:
            break
        sym = a[0]
        if sym in held or a[1] <= 0.0:
            continue
        asks = a[3].get("asks", [])
        px = a[2]
        if len(asks) > 0:
            px = asks[0][0]
        qty = int(20000.0 / px)
        if qty > 0 and cash > qty * px + 5000:
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty, price=px)
            cash = cash - qty * px
            slots = slots - 1
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=5000)

def byroc(a):
    return a[0]

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    if not snap["trading_day"]:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return
    rnd = snap["round"]
    tick = snap["tick"]

    if rnd >= 57:
        if state.get("derisk") != rnd:
            state["derisk"] = rnd
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            for p in b["positions"]:
                if p["quantity"] > 0:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=p["symbol"], quantity=p["quantity"])
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    if tick < 400 or state.get("day") == rnd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)
        return
    state["day"] = rnd

    q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for p in b["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p["quantity"]

    rows = []
    for r in q:
        h = api.call("stock:market", "history", symbol=r["symbol"], limit=4)
        if len(h) < 4 or h[-4] <= 0:
            continue
        roc = h[-1] / h[-4] - 1.0
        if roc > 0:
            rows.append([roc, r["symbol"], r["price"]])
    rows.sort(key=byroc, reverse=True)

    keep = []
    for x in rows[:6]:
        keep.append(x[1])

    for sym, qty in held.items():
        if qty > 0 and sym not in keep:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)

    cash = b["available_cash"]
    budget = 13000
    for x in rows[:6]:
        sym = x[1]
        if held.get(sym, 0) > 0:
            continue
        if cash < budget + 2000:
            continue
        px = x[2]
        qty = int(budget / px)
        if qty < 10:
            continue
        api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty, price=px * 1.02)
        cash -= qty * px

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=200)

def on_tick():
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    wealth = book["wealth"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p
    pending = []
    for o in api.call("stock:market", "orders"):
        if o.get("status") in ("placed", "partial", "open", "new"):
            pending.append(o.get("symbol"))

    # 卖出：成本止损 + 超仓减仓
    for r in quotes:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s not in held:
            continue
        p = held[s]
        qty = p["quantity"]
        pnl = p.get("pnl_pct", 0)
        px = r["price"]
        if s in pending:
            continue
        # 止损阈值随波动率放宽：高波动股给更大空间
        vol = r["volatility"]
        stop_pct = -8 if vol is not None and vol > 0.03 else -5
        if pnl is not None and pnl < stop_pct:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=s, quantity=qty, price=px * 0.98)
        elif qty * px > wealth * 0.25:
            excess = int((qty * px - wealth * 0.15) / px)
            if excess > 0:
                api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=s, quantity=excess, price=px * 0.99)

    # 选股：多头排列、动量为正
    cands = []
    for r in quotes:
        ma5 = r["ma5"]
        ma10 = r["ma10"]
        ma20 = r["ma20"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        vol = r["volatility"]
        if ma5 is None or ma10 is None or ma20 is None or mom is None or vol is None or vol <= 0:
            continue
        if ma5 > ma10 and ma10 >= ma20 and mom > 0 and r["price"] >= ma5:
            cands.append(r)

    def score(r):
        return r["momentum_5"] / (r["volatility"] + 0.001)
    cands.sort(key=score, reverse=True)
    cands = cands[:3]

    # 分批建仓：单股上限15%财富，每次60股
    for r in cands:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s in pending:
            continue
        cur_val = 0
        if s in held:
            cur_val = held[s]["quantity"] * r["price"]
        if cur_val >= wealth * 0.15:
            continue
        limit_px = r["price"] * 0.997
        if cash < limit_px * 60 or cash < 5000:
            continue
        api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=s, quantity=60, price=limit_px)
        cash = cash - limit_px * 60

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1200)

def on_tick():
    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    wealth = book["wealth"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p
    pending = []
    for o in api.call("stock:market", "orders"):
        if o.get("status") in ("placed", "partial", "open", "new"):
            pending.append(o.get("symbol"))
    # 当日卖出标记，避免同日回补
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    rd = snap.get("round", 0)
    if state.get("rd") != rd:
        state["rd"] = rd
        state["sold"] = []
    sold = state.get("sold", [])

    # 卖出：成本止损 + 超仓减仓
    for r in quotes:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s not in held:
            continue
        p = held[s]
        qty = p["quantity"]
        pnl = p.get("pnl_pct", 0)
        px = r["price"]
        if s in pending:
            continue
        # 止损阈值随波动率放宽：高波动股给更大空间
        vol = r["volatility"]
        stop_pct = -8 if vol is not None and vol > 0.03 else -5
        # 趋势转弱：动量转负且跌破5日线
        ma5 = r["ma5"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        trend_weak = (mom is not None and mom < 0 and ma5 is not None and px < ma5)
        if pnl is not None and pnl < stop_pct:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=s, quantity=qty, price=px * 0.98)
            if s not in sold:
                sold.append(s)
        elif trend_weak:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=s, quantity=qty, price=px * 0.99)
            if s not in sold:
                sold.append(s)
        elif qty * px > wealth * 0.25:
            excess = int((qty * px - wealth * 0.15) / px)
            if excess > 0:
                api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=s, quantity=excess, price=px * 0.99)
                if s not in sold:
                    sold.append(s)
    state["sold"] = sold

    # 选股：多头排列、动量为正
    cands = []
    for r in quotes:
        ma5 = r["ma5"]
        ma10 = r["ma10"]
        ma20 = r["ma20"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        vol = r["volatility"]
        if ma5 is None or ma10 is None or ma20 is None or mom is None or vol is None or vol <= 0:
            continue
        if ma5 > ma10 and ma10 >= ma20 and mom > 0 and r["price"] >= ma5:
            cands.append(r)

    def score(r):
        return r["momentum_5"] / (r["volatility"] + 0.001)
    cands.sort(key=score, reverse=True)
    cands = cands[:3]

    # 分批建仓：单股上限15%财富，每次60股
    for r in cands:
        s = r["symbol"]
        if s in pending or s in sold:
            continue
        cur_val = 0
        if s in held:
            cur_val = held[s]["quantity"] * r["price"]
        if cur_val >= wealth * 0.15:
            continue
        limit_px = r["price"] * 0.997
        if cash < limit_px * 60 or cash < 5000:
            continue
        api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=s, quantity=60, price=limit_px)
        cash = cash - limit_px * 60

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1200)

🗣 量化丁 脚本完整、逻辑清晰。总结本回合优化：
- 加入**趋势转弱主动减仓**（动量转负且跌破ma5），让资金从走弱股（如jinhui）腾出；
- 加入**当日卖出不回补**，避免同日买卖亏价差；
- 研究验证计算量11.5万步安全，选股前三为beiyuan、shanhai、hongfan。

提交本回合。

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    rd = snap["round"]
    if state.get("day") == rd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=5000)
        return
    state["day"] = rd
    q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p
    rows = []
    for r in q:
        ma = r["ma20"]
        px = r["price"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        if ma is None or ma == 0 or mom is None:
            continue
        score = 2.0 * (px / ma - 1.0) + 0.5 * mom
        rows.append([r["symbol"], score, px, r["depth"]])
    def ks(a):
        return a[1]
    rows.sort(key=ks, reverse=True)
    top4 = []
    for a in rows[:4]:
        top4.append(a[0])
    for sym in held:
        p = held[sym]
        aq = p["available_quantity"]
        if aq <= 0:
            continue
        if sym not in top4:
            px = p["price"]
            for a in rows:
                if a[0] == sym:
                    px = a[2]
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=aq, price=px * 0.99)
    nkeep = 0
    for sym in held:
        if sym in top4:
            nkeep = nkeep + 1
    slots = 4 - nkeep
    if slots < 0:
        slots = 0
    for a in rows:
        if slots <= 0:
            break
        sym = a[0]
        if sym in held or a[1] <= 0.0:
            continue
        asks = a[3].get("asks", [])
        px = a[2]
        if len(asks) > 0:
            px = asks[0][0]
        qty = int(22000.0 / px)
        if qty > 0 and cash > qty * px + 3000:
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty, price=px)
            cash = cash - qty * px
            slots = slots - 1
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=5000)

🗣 量化甲 The strategy is correct. Let me end the turn.

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    tick = snap["tick"]
    rnd = snap["round"]

    if tick > 1200:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)
        return
    if state.get("last_round", 0) == rnd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)
        return
    state["last_round"] = rnd

    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for row in book["positions"]:
        held[row["symbol"]] = row["quantity"]

    scores = []
    for row in quotes:
        s = 0.0
        for n in row["news"]:
            s = s + n["cashflow_change_pct"]
        scores.append([s, row["symbol"], row["price"]])

    def by_score(a):
        return a[0]

    scores.sort(key=by_score, reverse=True)

    rank = {}
    for i in range(len(scores)):
        rank[scores[i][1]] = i

    for sym in held:
        if rank.get(sym, 99) >= 8:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])

    cash = book["available_cash"]
    for i in range(min(5, len(scores))):
        sym = scores[i][1]
        p = scores[i][2]
        if scores[i][0] <= 0:
            continue
        if sym in held:
            continue
        if cash < 8000:
            continue
        qty = int(9000 / p)
        if qty >= 1:
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
            cash = cash - qty * p

    print("r=" + str(rnd) + " top=" + str(scores[0][1]) + " held=" + str(len(held)))
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    rd = snap["round"]
    if state.get("day") != rd:
        state["day"] = rd
        state["bought"] = []
    q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p
    rows = []
    for r in q:
        ma = r["ma20"]
        px = r["price"]
        mom = r["momentum_5"]
        if ma is None or ma == 0 or mom is None:
            continue
        score = 2.0 * (px / ma - 1.0) + 0.5 * mom
        rows.append([r["symbol"], score, px, r["depth"]])
    def ks(a):
        return a[1]
    rows.sort(key=ks, reverse=True)
    top4 = []
    for a in rows[:4]:
        top4.append(a[0])
    for sym in held:
        p = held[sym]
        aq = p["available_quantity"]
        if aq <= 0 or sym in top4:
            continue
        px = p["price"]
        for a in rows:
            if a[0] == sym:
                bids = a[3].get("bids", [])
                if len(bids) > 0:
                    px = bids[0][0]
        api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=aq, price=px)
    bought = state["bought"]
    for a in rows[:4]:
        sym = a[0]
        if sym in held or sym in bought or a[1] <= 0.0:
            continue
        asks = a[3].get("asks", [])
        px = a[2]
        if len(asks) > 0:
            px = asks[0][0]
        px = px * 1.01
        qty = int(22000.0 / px)
        if qty > 0 and cash > qty * px + 3000:
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty, price=px)
            cash = cash - qty * px
            bought.append(sym)
    state["bought"] = bought
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=2000)

🗣 量化甲 The strategy is correct and will now: sell taiping/qingzhou (out of top-4), keep yundu/shanhai/beiyuan, and add hongfan — all persistent uptrend leaders. Sells re-place at the bid every 2000 ticks using `available_quantity` (no double-sell), buys cross the spread at ask×1.01 for reliable fills. Let me end the turn.

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    tick = snap["tick"]
    rnd = snap["round"]

    if tick > 1200:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)
        return
    if state.get("last_round", 0) == rnd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)
        return
    state["last_round"] = rnd

    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for row in book["positions"]:
        held[row["symbol"]] = row["quantity"]

    scores = []
    for row in quotes:
        s = 0.0
        for n in row["news"]:
            s = s + n["cashflow_change_pct"]
        scores.append([s, row["symbol"], row["price"]])

    def by_score(a):
        return a[0]

    scores.sort(key=by_score, reverse=True)

    rank = {}
    for i in range(len(scores)):
        rank[scores[i][1]] = i

    for sym in held:
        if rank.get(sym, 99) >= 8:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])

    cash = book["available_cash"]
    for i in range(min(5, len(scores))):
        sym = scores[i][1]
        p = scores[i][2]
        if scores[i][0] <= 0:
            continue
        if sym in held:
            continue
        if cash < 8000:
            continue
        qty = int(9000 / p)
        if qty >= 1:
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
            cash = cash - qty * p

    print("r=" + str(rnd) + " top=" + str(scores[0][1]) + " held=" + str(len(held)))
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)

- Market has consistent positive drift (~1%/day), and avoiding decliners raises that to ~1.8%/day

**Critical realization**: v17 has **never run on a trading day** (rounds 6-7 were rest days). I've been rewriting the strategy repeatedly without ever getting clean execution data. The disciplined move is to let it run unchanged, verify it executes correctly, then optimize based on real results.

[9·行动] 量化甲 ◇ 读取脚本 → 槽 1 当前源码（第 18 版）：
def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    rd = snap["round"]
    if state.get("day") == rd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=10000)
        return
    state["day"] = rd
    q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    equity = book["wealth"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p
    rows = []
    for r in q:
        ma = r["ma20"]
        px = r["price"]
        anc = r["anchor"]
        if ma is None or ma == 0 or anc is None or anc == 0:
            continue
        score = 2.0 * (px / anc - 1.0) + (px / ma - 1.0)
        rows.append([r["symbol"], score, px, r["depth"]])
    def ks(a):
        return a[1]
    rows.sort(key=ks, reverse=True)
    top4 = []
    for a in rows[:4]:
        top4.append(a[0])
    info = {}
    for a in rows:
        info[a[0]] = a
    for sym in held:
        if sym in top4:
            continue
        p = held[sym]
        aq = p["available_quantity"]
        if aq <= 0:
            continue
        px = p["price"]
        a = info.get(sym)
        if a is not None:
            bids = a[3].get("bids", [])
            if len(bids) > 0:
                px = bids[0][0]
        api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=aq, price=px)
    target = equity / 4.0
    for sym in top4:
        a = info.get(sym)
        if a is None:
            continue
        px = a[2]
        asks = a[3].get("asks", [])
        if len(asks) > 0:
            px = asks[0][0]
        buy_px = px * 1.01
        cur = 0.0
        if sym in held:
            cur = held[sym]["quantity"] * px
        if cur >= target * 0.85:
            continue
        want = target - cur
        if want > cash - 2000.0:
            want = cash - 2000.0
        if want <= 0:
            continue
        qty = int(want / buy_px)
        if qty > 0:
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty, price=buy_px)
            cash = cash - qty * buy_px
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=10000)

🗣 量化甲 The strategy is correct. It ranks by long-term trend (price vs anchor, weighted 2×, plus price vs ma20), which currently selects shanhai, yundu, lanhai, and beiyuan — the persistent winners. It rebalances all four to equal weight (~23.7k each), deploying full capital, and sells anything outside the top 4. With pool 2 bought, it won't hit the compute limit. Let me end the turn.

[9·行动] 量化丙 ◇ 读取脚本 → 槽 1 当前源码（第 10 版）：
def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    if not snap["trading_day"]:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return
    rnd = snap["round"]
    tick = snap["tick"]

    if rnd >= 57:
        if state.get("derisk") != rnd:
            state["derisk"] = rnd
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            for p in b["positions"]:
                if p["quantity"] > 0:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=p["symbol"], quantity=p["quantity"])
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    if tick >= 3500 and tick < 4500:
        if state.get("sold") != rnd:
            state["sold"] = rnd
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            for p in b["positions"]:
                if p["quantity"] > 0:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=p["symbol"], quantity=p["quantity"])
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    if tick >= 18500:
        if state.get("bought") != rnd:
            state["bought"] = rnd
            q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            cash = b["available_cash"]
            n = len(q)
            if n > 0:
                per = cash * 0.96 / n
                for r in q:
                    px = r["price"]
                    if px <= 0:
                        continue
                    qty = int(per / px)
                    if qty >= 10:
                        api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=r["symbol"], quantity=qty, price=px * 1.01)
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)

🗣 量化丙 The evidence is clear: momentum fails, mean reversion works, and there's a strong intraday spike-then-fade pattern. My current momentum strategy lost 3.3% in one day. def byret(a):
    return a[0]

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    if not snap["trading_day"]:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return
    rnd = snap["round"]
    tick = snap["tick"]

    if rnd >= 57:
        if state.get("derisk") != rnd:
            state["derisk"] = rnd
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            for p in b["positions"]:
                if p["quantity"] > 0:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=p["symbol"], quantity=p["quantity"])
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    if tick >= 2500 and tick < 3500:
        if state.get("sold") != rnd:
            state["sold"] = rnd
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            for p in b["positions"]:
                if p["quantity"] > 0:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=p["symbol"], quantity=p["quantity"])
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    if tick >= 18500:
        if state.get("bought") != rnd:
            state["bought"] = rnd
            q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            cash = b["available_cash"]
            rows = []
            for r in q:
                p = r["path"]
                if len(p) < 5 or p[0] <= 0 or r["price"] <= 0:
                    continue
                ret = p[-1] / p[0] - 1.0
                rows.append([ret, r["symbol"], r["price"]])
            rows.sort(key=byret)
            n = min(5, len(rows))
            if n > 0:
                per = cash * 0.95 / n
                for x in rows[:n]:
                    qty = int(per / x[2])
                    if qty >= 10:
                        api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=x[1], quantity=qty, price=x[2] * 1.01)
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)

🗣 量化丙 Let me refine the sell timing based on the intraday paths — the peaks occur around points 3-8 of 20, i.e., roughly ticks 3000-8000, so selling at 2500-3500 is slightly too early.

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    tick = snap["tick"]
    rnd = snap["round"]

    if tick > 1200:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)
        return
    if state.get("last_round", 0) == rnd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)
        return
    state["last_round"] = rnd

    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for row in book["positions"]:
        held[row["symbol"]] = row["quantity"]

    scores = []
    for row in quotes:
        s = 0.0
        for n in row["news"]:
            s = s + n["cashflow_change_pct"]
        scores.append([s, row["symbol"], row["price"]])

    def by_score(a):
        return a[0]

    scores.sort(key=by_score, reverse=True)

    rank = {}
    for i in range(len(scores)):
        rank[scores[i][1]] = i

    for sym in held:
        if rank.get(sym, 99) >= 9:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])

    cash = book["available_cash"]
    for i in range(min(8, len(scores))):
        sym = scores[i][1]
        p = scores[i][2]
        if scores[i][0] <= 0:
            continue
        if sym in held:
            continue
        if cash < 6000:
            continue
        qty = int(10000 / p)
        if qty >= 1:
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
            cash = cash - qty * p

    print("r=" + str(rnd) + " top=" + str(scores[0][1]) + " held=" + str(len(held)))
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)

[10·行动] 量化丙 ◇ 读取脚本 → 槽 1 当前源码（第 14 版）：
def byret(a):
    return a[0]

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    if not snap["trading_day"]:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return
    rnd = snap["round"]
    tick = snap["tick"]

    if rnd >= 57:
        if state.get("derisk") != rnd:
            state["derisk"] = rnd
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            for p in b["positions"]:
                if p["quantity"] > 0:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=p["symbol"], quantity=p["quantity"])
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    if tick >= 9000 and tick < 11000:
        if state.get("sold") != rnd:
            state["sold"] = rnd
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            for p in b["positions"]:
                if p["quantity"] > 0:
                    api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=p["symbol"], quantity=p["quantity"])
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    if tick >= 19000:
        if state.get("bought") != rnd:
            state["bought"] = rnd
            q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
            b = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
            cash = b["available_cash"]
            rows = []
            for r in q:
                p = r["path"]
                if len(p) < 5 or p[0] <= 0 or r["price"] <= 0:
                    continue
                ret = p[-1] / p[0] - 1.0
                rows.append([ret, r["symbol"], r["price"]])
            rows.sort(key=byret)
            n = min(5, len(rows))
            if n > 0:
                per = cash * 0.95 / n
                for x in rows[:n]:
                    qty = int(per / x[2])
                    if qty >= 10:
                        api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=x[1], quantity=qty, price=x[2] * 1.01)
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)
        return

    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=500)

🗣 量化丙 The strategy is saved correctly. Let me end the turn.

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    rd = snap["round"]
    if state.get("day") == rd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=10000)
        return
    state["day"] = rd
    q = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    cash = book["available_cash"]
    equity = book["wealth"]
    held = {}
    for p in book["positions"]:
        held[p["symbol"]] = p
    rows = []
    for r in q:
        ma = r["ma20"]
        px = r["price"]
        anc = r["anchor"]
        if ma is None or ma == 0 or anc is None or anc == 0:
            continue
        score = 2.0 * (px / anc - 1.0) + (px / ma - 1.0)
        if r["symbol"] in held:
            score = score + 0.05
        rows.append([r["symbol"], score, px, r["depth"]])
    def ks(a):
        return a[1]
    rows.sort(key=ks, reverse=True)
    top4 = []
    for a in rows[:4]:
        top4.append(a[0])
    info = {}
    for a in rows:
        info[a[0]] = a
    for sym in held:
        if sym in top4:
            continue
        p = held[sym]
        aq = p["available_quantity"]
        if aq <= 0:
            continue
        px = p["price"]
        a = info.get(sym)
        if a is not None:
            bids = a[3].get("bids", [])
            if len(bids) > 0:
                px = bids[0][0]
        api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=aq, price=px)
    target = equity / 4.0
    for sym in top4:
        a = info.get(sym)
        if a is None:
            continue
        px = a[2]
        asks = a[3].get("asks", [])
        if len(asks) > 0:
            px = asks[0][0]
        buy_px = px * 1.01
        cur = 0.0
        if sym in held:
            cur = held[sym]["quantity"] * px
        if cur >= target * 0.85:
            continue
        want = target - cur
        if want > cash - 2000.0:
            want = cash - 2000.0
        if want <= 0:
            continue
        qty = int(want / buy_px)
        if qty > 0:
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty, price=buy_px)
            cash = cash - qty * buy_px
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=10000)

🗣 量化甲 The strategy is correct. With the 0.05 stickiness bonus, current holdings (yundu 0.96, shanhai 0.67, lanhai 0.45, beiyuan 0.45) stay ahead of the marginal challengers (tianhe 0.42, jinhui 0.41), avoiding needless churn while genuine breakouts can still trigger rotation. Let me end the turn.

- So **trading P&L is roughly -950 (essentially flat)** while the market rose ~4-5% over the same period

v20 does exactly this: equal-weight all stocks above anchor (~9 stocks), sell only if they drop below anchor. It's cheap enough to run on the free tier (round 11's similar script used only ~2,560 steps/callback), so **no compute purchase needed**. Let me verify and commit.

def on_tick():
    snap = api.call("stock:clock", "snapshot")
    tick = snap["tick"]
    rnd = snap["round"]

    if tick > 1200:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)
        return
    if state.get("last_round", 0) == rnd:
        api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)
        return
    state["last_round"] = rnd

    quotes = api.call("stock:market", "quotes")
    book = api.call("stock:portfolio", "holdings")
    held = {}
    for row in book["positions"]:
        held[row["symbol"]] = row["quantity"]

    prices = {}
    good = []
    for row in quotes:
        sym = row["symbol"]
        p = row["price"]
        a = row["anchor"]
        prices[sym] = p
        if p is None or a is None or a <= 0 or p <= 0:
            continue
        if row.get("delist_review_round") is not None:
            continue
        if p > a:
            good.append(sym)

    for sym in held:
        if sym not in good:
            api.call("stock:market", "sell", symbol=sym, quantity=held[sym])

    cash = book["available_cash"]
    per = 9000
    bought = 0
    for sym in good:
        if sym in held:
            continue
        if cash < per + 500:
            continue
        p = prices[sym]
        qty = int(per / p)
        if qty >= 1:
            api.call("stock:market", "buy", symbol=sym, quantity=qty)
            cash = cash - qty * p
            bought = bought + 1

    print("r=" + str(rnd) + " good=" + str(len(good)) + " held=" + str(len(held)) + " B=" + str(bought))
    api.call("stock:clock", "sleep", ticks=1000)

🗣 量化乙 I've committed to v20. Let me end the turn — no purchases this round, as the script is cheap enough for the free tier.
